namespace = POD_chargen

scripted_trigger POD_is_landed_or_valid_for_landless = {
	trigger_if = {
		limit = { trait:$TRAIT$ = { has_trait_flag = landless_unavailable } }
		is_landed = yes
	}
}

scripted_effect POD_setup_faith_selection_event = {
	set_variable = {
		name  = chargen_religion
		value = $RELIGION$
	}
	custom_tooltip = POD_chargen_faith_selection
}

scripted_effect POD_trigger_faith_selection_event = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = chargen_religion }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name  = next_event
			value = event_id:$NEXT_EVENT$
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable_list = chargen_faiths }
			clear_variable_list = chargen_faiths
		}
		var:chargen_religion = {
			ordered_faith = {
				order_by = num_county_followers
				min = 0
				max = 100
				check_range_bounds = no
				root = {
					add_to_variable_list = {
						name   = chargen_faiths
						target = prev
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ordered_in_list = {
			variable = chargen_faiths
			position = 0
			prev = {
				set_variable = {
					name  = chargen_faith_selection
					value = prev
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.6000
	}
	else = {
		trigger_event = $NEXT_EVENT$
	}
}


POD_chargen.1 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1.desc

	theme = caitiff
	left_portrait = root
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}
	
	
	immediate = {
		play_music_cue = "mood_in_search_of_iniquity"
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1.option.a
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1.option.a.tt
		add_character_flag = POD_used_character_generator
		hidden_effect = {
			set_character_faith = faith:viahumanitatis
			
			set_local_variable = {
				name = saved_immortal_age
				value = POD_visual_age
			}
			
			POD_remove_all_supernatural_atribute_effect = yes
			remove_character_flag = immune_to_disease
			remove_character_flag = had_POD_character_initialization

			save_scope_as = character_being_changed
			
			if = {
				limit = { is_from_ruler_designer = yes }
				house = { save_scope_as = coa_target }
			}
			else = {
				POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
				create_character = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = created_vampirelord
					location = scope:character_being_changed.capital_province
					age = 1200
					culture = scope:character_being_changed.culture
					faith = scope:character_being_changed.faith
					dynasty = generate
					gender = male
					random_traits = yes
				}	
				
				set_house = scope:created_vampirelord.house 
				
				scope:created_vampirelord = { 
					death = {
						death_reason = death_mysterious
					}
				}
				POD_remove_parent_relation_effect = yes
			}
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
		}		
		
		if = {
			limit = { is_from_ruler_designer = no }
			trigger_event = {
				id = podintroductions.6000
				days = 3
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1.option.b
		
		POD_after_intro_initiation_effect = yes
	}	
	
}

POD_chargen.510 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.510.t
	desc = POD_chargen.510.desc

	theme = family
	left_portrait = root
	right_portrait = scope:house_head
	
	immediate = {
			house.house_head = {
				save_scope_as = house_head
			}
			house.house_head = {
				save_scope_as = vampiresire
			}			
			house = {
				save_scope_as = house
			}	
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.510.option.a
		
		house.house_head = {
			save_scope_as = vampiresire
		}
			
		root = {
			save_scope_as = vampirechilde
		}			
		root = {
			save_scope_as = created_vampirechilde
		}
	
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:house_head = {
					is_female = no
				}
			}
			set_father = scope:house_head
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:house_head = {
					is_female = yes
					}
			}
			set_mother = scope:house_head
		}

		scope:created_vampirechilde = {
			POD_character_vampire_inherit_generation_effect = yes	
		}
		
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.610 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.510.option.b
		add_trait = vampgeneration10

		# POD_add_house_heritage_modifier_effect = yes

		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.610 }
		}	
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.510.option.c
		add_trait = vampgeneration7
		
		POD_create_cadet_branch_effect = yes
		POD_add_house_heritage_modifier_effect = yes

		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.610 }
		}	
	}
	
}

# Blood Potency
POD_chargen.620 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.620.t
	desc = POD_chargen.620.desc

	theme = vampire
	left_portrait = root
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.620.option.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_blood_potency_trigger = no
			}
			POD_character_vampire_bloodpotency_initialization_effect = yes
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.650 }
	}
}

# The Masquerade
POD_chargen.650 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.650.t
	desc = POD_chargen.650.desc

	theme = masquerade
	left_portrait = root
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.650.option.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_masquerade_risk_trigger = no
			}
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = masquerade0_modifier 
			}
			#set_variable = { name = masquerade_level value = 5 }
		}			
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.670 }
	}
}

# The Hunger and The Beast
POD_chargen.670 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.670.t
	desc = POD_chargen.670.desc

	theme = vampire
	left_portrait = root
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_disease_trait_effect = yes
			add_character_flag = immune_to_disease
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.670.option.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_blood_hunger_trigger = no
			}
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = bloodhunger1_modifier
			}

POD_chargen.1000 = { 
	hidden = yes
	
	trigger = {
		has_trait = vampire
		has_predator_type_trigger = no
	}
		
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = ventrue }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.1100 #Ventrue Feeding Preferences
		}
		else = {
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.1010 #Predator types
		}
	}
}

POD_chargen.1010 = { #Predator types
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1010.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1010.desc

	theme = vampire
	left_portrait = root
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}

	option = { #Farmer
		name = POD_chargen.1010.option.a
		trigger = {
			is_ventrue_trigger = no
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = bloodpotency0_modifier
				has_character_modifier = bloodpotency1_modifier
				AND = {
					has_animalism_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						has_character_modifier = bloodpotency2_modifier
						has_character_modifier = bloodpotency3_modifier
						has_character_modifier = bloodpotency4_modifier
					}
				}
			}
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency4_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency5_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency6_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency7_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency8_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency9_modifier }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = bloodpotency10_modifier }
		}
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_farmer
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}
			add_piety = major_piety_gain
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_hunter }
				}
				add_trait = lifestyle_hunter
			}		
			add_animal_lifestyle_xp = minor_lifestyle_xp		
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_animalism_trigger = yes
					has_protean_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = protean
			}
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_animalism_trigger = no 
				}
				add_trait = animalism
			}
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
		# Ends CharGen
	}

	option = { #Human Predator Types
		name = POD_chargen.1010.option.b
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1020 }
	}
	
	option = { #Bloodleech
		name = POD_chargen.1010.option.c
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_bloodleech
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}
			add_prowess_skill = 1
			POD_bloodpotency_increase_effect = yes
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_celerity_trigger = yes
					has_protean_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = protean
			}
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_celerity_trigger = no 
				}
				add_trait = celerity
			}
		}		
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }	
	}	
}

POD_chargen.1030 = { #Charm
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1030.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1030.desc

	theme = vampire
	left_portrait = root
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}

	option = { #Siren
		name = POD_chargen.1030.option.a
		trigger = {
			is_gargoyle_trigger = no
			OR = {
				is_cappadocian_trigger = no
				POD_suppress_visual_clan_curse_trigger = yes
			}
			OR = {
				is_nosferatu_trigger = no
				POD_is_good_looking_as_nosferatu_trigger = yes
			}
			NOT = { has_trait = beauty_bad }
		}
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_siren
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}
			add_diplomacy_skill = 1
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT  = { has_trait = beauty_good }
				}
				add_trait = beauty_good_1
			}
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_presence_trigger = yes
					has_fortitude_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = fortitude
			}
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_presence_trigger = no 
				}
				add_trait = presence
			}		
			if = { 	
				limit = {
					has_fortitude_trigger = yes
					}
				add_melancholy_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
				}
			if = { 	
				limit = {
					has_presence_trigger = yes
					}
				add_sanguine_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
	}

	option = { #Gentry
		name = POD_chargen.1030.option.b
		trigger = {
			is_gargoyle_trigger = no
			OR = {
				is_nosferatu_trigger = no
				POD_is_good_looking_as_nosferatu_trigger = yes
			}
		}

			type = secret_predatortype_gentry
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}

			#POD_create_herd_effect = yes
			#custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1030.option.b.herd
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_dominate_trigger = yes
					has_presence_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = presence
			}
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_dominate_trigger = no 
				}
				add_trait = dominate
			}
			if = { 	
				limit = {
					has_dominate_trigger = yes
					}
				add_phlegmatic_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
				}
			if = { 	
				limit = {
					has_presence_trigger = yes
					}
				add_sanguine_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}	
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
	}
	
	option = { #Cultist
		name = POD_chargen.1030.option.c
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_cultist
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}
			#add_diplomacy_skill = 1
			add_piety = medium_piety_gain
			
			#hidden_effect = {
			#	POD_create_herd_effect = yes
			#	POD_create_herd_effect = yes
			#}
			custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1030.option.c.herd

					}

			}	
			if = {

					has_bloodsorcery_trigger = yes
				}

POD_chargen.1040 = { #Deceit
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1040.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1040.desc

	theme = vampire
	left_portrait = root
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}

	option = { #Cauchemar
		name = POD_chargen.1040.option.a
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_cauchemar
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}
			add_intrigue_skill = 2
			add_gold = minor_gold_value
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_obfuscate_trigger = yes
					has_auspex_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = auspex
			}
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_obfuscate_trigger = no 
				}
				add_trait = obfuscate
			}	
			if = { 	
				limit = {
					has_obfuscate_trigger = yes
				}
				add_melancholy_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
			if = { 	
				limit = {
					has_auspex_trigger = yes
				}
				add_phlegmatic_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}	
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
	}

	option = { #Familias
		name = POD_chargen.1040.option.b
		trigger = { is_ventrue_trigger = no }
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_familias
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}
			add_diplomacy_skill = 1
			#Spawn herd family
			hidden_effect = {
				POD_create_herd_effect = yes
				if = {
					limit = { exists = scope:herd }
					if = {
						limit = { scope:herd = { is_male = yes } }
						scope:herd = { save_scope_as = father }
						create_character = {
							location = root.capital_province
							template = POD_herd_young_woman_character
							save_scope_as = mother
						}
						POD_herdify_effect = { TARGET = scope:mother }
					}
					else = {
						scope:herd = { save_scope_as = mother }
						create_character = {
							location = root.capital_province
							template = POD_herd_young_man_character
							save_scope_as = father
						}
						POD_herdify_effect = { TARGET = scope:father }
					}
					scope:father = { marry = scope:mother }
					while = {
						count = 2
						create_character = {
							location = root.capital_province
							template = POD_herd_child_character
							save_scope_as = child
						}
						scope:child = {
							set_father = scope:father
							set_mother = scope:mother
						}
						POD_herdify_effect = { TARGET = scope:child }
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1040.option.b.herd
			#Add herd hook to generated family
			custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.1040.option.b.tt
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_modifier = masquerade0_modifier
				}
				POD_masquerade_exposure_increase_effect = yes
			}
			add_phlegmatic_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_dominate_trigger = yes
					has_animalism_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = animalism
			}
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_dominate_trigger = no 
				}
				add_trait = dominate
			}		
			if = { 	
				limit = {
					has_dominate_trigger = yes
					}
				add_phlegmatic_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
	}
	
	option = { #Footpad
		name = POD_chargen.1040.option.c
		trigger = {
			is_ventrue_trigger = no
		}
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_footpad
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}
			add_intrigue_skill = 3
			add_prestige = minor_prestige_loss
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_obfuscate_trigger = yes
					has_potence_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = potence
			}
			if = { 	
				limit = {
					has_obfuscate_trigger = yes
				}
				add_melancholy_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
			if = { 	
				limit = {
					has_potence_trigger = yes
				}
				add_choleric_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}		
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_obfuscate_trigger = no 
				}
				add_trait = obfuscate
			}
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
	}		
	
	option = { #Non-Human Predator Types
		name = POD_chargen.1040.option.d
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1020 }
	}
}

POD_chargen.1060 = { #Violence
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1060.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1060.desc

	theme = vampire
	left_portrait = root
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_shader_event_fog_alley_night"
		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}

	option = { #Alleycat
		name = POD_chargen.1060.option.a
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_alleycat
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}
			add_prowess_skill = 3
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_potence_trigger = yes
					has_celerity_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = celerity
			}
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_potence_trigger = no 
				}
				add_trait = potence
			}		
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
	}
	
	option = { #Extortionist
		name = POD_chargen.1060.option.b
		add_secret = {
			type = secret_predatortype_extortionist
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = POD_change_predatortype_flag }
			}
			add_prowess_skill = 2
			add_intrigue_skill = 1
			add_gold = minor_gold_value
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_dominate_trigger = yes
					has_potence_trigger = no
				}
				add_trait = potence
			}
			if = { 
				limit = {
					has_dominate_trigger = no 
				}
				add_trait = dominate
			}	
			if = { 	
				limit = {
					has_potence_trigger = yes
					has_dominate_trigger = no
				}
				add_choleric_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
			if = { 	
				limit = {
					has_dominate_trigger = yes
					has_potence_trigger = no
				}
				add_phlegmatic_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}		
			if = { 	
				limit = {
					has_potence_trigger = yes
					has_dominate_trigger = yes
				}
				add_choleric_lifestyle_xp = medium_lifestyle_xp
			}
		}
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1200 }
	}
	
	option = { #Non-Human Predator Types
		name = POD_chargen.1050.option.e
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1020 }
	}	
}

		add_trait = fortitude
		add_character_flag = POD_initial_revenant_disciplines_set
		POD_character_revenant_faith_initialization_effect = yes

		add_trait = fortitude
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_trait = wrathful }
				has_trait = calm
			}
			random = {
				chance = 75
				add_trait = wrathful
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_trait = cannibal }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_secret = {
					type = secret_cannibal
				}
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = POD_initial_revenant_disciplines_set
		POD_character_revenant_faith_initialization_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1360 }
		}		
	}	

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1310.option.e
		set_house = house:house_premysl
		add_trait = auspex
		add_trait = potence
		add_trait = vicissitude

		POD_character_revenant_faith_initialization_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {

POD_chargen.1360 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1360.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1360.desc

	theme = family
	left_portrait = root
	right_portrait = scope:house_head
	
	immediate = {
			house.house_head = {
				save_scope_as = house_head
			}		
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1360.option.a		
		trigger = {
			age < scope:house_head.age
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:house_head = {
					is_female = no
				}
			}
			set_father = scope:house_head
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:house_head = {
					is_female = yes
					}
			}
			set_mother = scope:house_head
		}
		
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1370 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1360.option.b
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1370}
		}		
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1360.option.c
		POD_create_cadet_branch_effect = yes	

		#Add house heritage modifier check here

		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1370}
		}		
	}
	
#	option = 
#	{
#		name = POD_chargen.1360.option.b			
#		hidden_effect = {
#			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1370 }
#		}	
#	}
	
}

	left_portrait = root

		container = "background_shader_vfx_container"
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1370.option.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_masquerade_risk_trigger = no
			}
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = masquerade0_modifier 
			}
		}	
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1380 }
		}
	}
}

#Inquisitors order choice

POD_chargen.1400 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1400.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1400.desc

	theme = inquisition
	left_portrait = root
	
	immediate = {	
	

		character:791707 = {
			save_scope_as = gauthier
		}
		character:791708 = {
			save_scope_as = leopold
		}	
		character:791713 = {
			save_scope_as = maria
		}
		character:791715 = {
			save_scope_as = vittoria
		}	
		character:791723 = {
			save_scope_as = aignen
		}
		character:791725 = {
			save_scope_as = rodrigue
		}			
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.a

		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		set_house = scope:gauthier.house

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}
		POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
		POD_remove_spouse_relation_effect = yes
		
		if = {
			limit = { 
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = { 
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
	}
			
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.b
		
		set_house = scope:rodrigue.house

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}	
		POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
		POD_remove_spouse_relation_effect = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.c
		
		trigger = { is_female = yes }
		
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		
		set_house = scope:maria.house
		
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}
		POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
		POD_remove_spouse_relation_effect = yes

		if = {
			limit = { 
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = { 
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.d
		
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		
		set_house = scope:aignen.house
		
		if = {
			limit = { 
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = { 
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.e

		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		
		set_house = scope:vittoria.house
		
		if = {
			limit = { 
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = { 
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}
		POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
		POD_remove_spouse_relation_effect = yes
		
		if = {
			limit = { 
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = { 
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
		
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.f
		
		set_house = scope:leopold.house

		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		
		
		if = {
			limit = { 
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = { 
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.g
		
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1410 }
	}	

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.h
		
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.1500 }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1400.option.i
		
		remove_trait = supehunter
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
	}
	
	after = {
		POD_reset_grand_cities_on_splat_change = yes
	}
}			

POD_chargen.1410 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.1410.t
	desc = POD_chargen.1410.desc

	theme = inquisition
	left_portrait = root

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1410.option.a
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		if = {
			limit = { 
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = { 
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = order_member }
			remove_trait = order_member
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 
				has_trait = devoted
			}
		remove_trait = devoted
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			POD_give_orison_effect = yes
		}
		dynasty = { add_dynasty_modifier = custom_lay_order_modifier }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1410.option.b
		
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		if = {
			limit = { 
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = { 
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}
		POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
		POD_remove_spouse_relation_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			POD_give_orison_effect = yes
		}
		dynasty = { add_dynasty_modifier = custom_mendicant_order_modifier }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1410.option.c
		
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		if = {
			limit = { 
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = { 
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}
		POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
		POD_remove_spouse_relation_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			POD_give_orison_effect = yes
		}
		dynasty = { add_dynasty_modifier = custom_knightly_order_modifier }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1410.option.d
		
		set_character_faith = faith:shadowinquisition
		if = {
			limit = { 
				POD_has_true_faith_trigger = no
				OR = { 
					POD_is_infernalist_trigger = no
					POD_splat_mixing_haight_mode = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = truefaith
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = order_member } }
			add_trait = order_member
		}
		POD_remove_child_relation_effect = yes
		POD_remove_spouse_relation_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			POD_give_orison_effect = yes
		}
		dynasty = { add_dynasty_modifier = custom_contemplative_order_modifier }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.1410.option.e
		
		trigger = {
			exists = scope:character_being_changed
		}
		
		remove_trait = supehunter
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
	}
}

		animation = idle
	}

	}

	}

# Werewolf
POD_chargen.2600 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.2600.t
	desc = POD_chargen.2600.desc

	theme = witchcraft
	left_portrait = root

	immediate = {
		play_music_cue = "mood_lone_wolf"
	}
	
	trigger = { POD_has_breed_trigger = no }

	on_trigger_fail = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2700
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2600.option.a
		
		add_trait = homid_born
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2700
	}
			
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2600.option.b
		
		add_trait = beast_born
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2700
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2600.option.d
		
		trigger = { exists = scope:character_being_changed }
		
		remove_trait = werewolf
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2
	}
}

	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.2650.t
	desc = POD_chargen.2650.desc

	theme = witchcraft
	left_portrait = root
	
	trigger = { POD_has_entomos_clade_trigger = no }

	on_trigger_fail = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = ananasi }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.2651
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { POD_is_cets_trigger = yes }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.2653
		}
		else = {
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.2652
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_ananasi
		
		add_trait = ananasi
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_eshieho
		
		add_trait = eshieho
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_mishaho
		
		add_trait = mishaho
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_bahaesh
		
		add_trait = bahaesh
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_ishihambi
		
		add_trait = ishihambi
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_iqhawe
		
		add_trait = iqhawe
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_umakhi
		
		add_trait = umakhi
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_umlimi
		
		add_trait = umlimi
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_umbulali
		
		add_trait = umbulali
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_umhloli
		
		add_trait = umhloli
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_injiki
		
		add_trait = injiki
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_kstyw
		
		add_trait = kstyw
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2650.option.n
		
		trigger = { exists = scope:character_being_changed }
		
		remove_trait = entomos
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2
	}

	after = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = ananasi }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.2651
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { POD_is_cets_trigger = yes }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.2653
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = entomos }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.2652
		}
	}
}

POD_chargen.2701 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.2701.t
	desc = POD_chargen.2701.desc

	theme = witchcraft
	left_portrait = root
	
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			POD_has_auspice_trigger = no 
			POD_has_wrong_ajaba_auspice_trigger = yes 
		}
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2801
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { POD_has_wrong_ajaba_auspice_trigger = yes }
			POD_remove_auspice_traits_effect = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2701.option.a
		
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_female = yes
				has_trait = metis
			}
		}
		
		add_trait = ahroun
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2701.option.b
		
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_female = no
				has_trait = metis
			}
		}
		
		add_trait = galliard
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2701.option.c
		
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_female = yes
				has_trait = metis
			}
		}
		
		add_trait = ragabash
	}
			
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2701.option.d
		
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_female = no
				has_trait = metis
			}
		}
		
		add_trait = theurge
	}

	after = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2801
	}
}

POD_chargen.2800 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.2800.t
	desc = POD_chargen.2800.desc

	theme = witchcraft
	left_portrait = root
	
	trigger = {
		is_a_cultist = no
		POD_is_supehunter_trigger = no
		NOR = {
			religion = { is_in_family = rf_wyrm }
			religion = { is_in_family = rf_gaia }
		}
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2814
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2800.option.a
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:blackfury_religion }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2800.option.b
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:bonegnawer_religion }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2800.option.c
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:childofgaia_religion }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2800.option.d
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:fianna_religion }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2800.option.e
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:getoffenris_religion }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2800.option.f
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:redtalon_religion }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2800.option.g
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:shadowlord_religion }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2800.option.h
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:silentstrider_religion }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2800.option.i
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:silverfang_religion }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2800.option.j
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:stargazer_religion }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2800.option.k
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:warderofmen_religion }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2800.option.l
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:blackspiraldancer_religion }
	}
	
	after = {
		POD_trigger_faith_selection_event = { NEXT_EVENT = POD_chargen.2814 }
		#trigger_event = POD_chargen.2814
		#add_character_modifier = { modifier = POD_free_faith_change_modifier days = 1 }
	}
}

# Demon
POD_chargen.2901 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_demon_maintenance.10.t
	desc = POD_demon_maintenance.10.desc
	theme = demon

	left_portrait = {
		character = root
		animation = idle
	}

	immediate = {
		play_music_cue = "mood_discordant"
	}
	
	trigger = {
		has_trait = demon
		OR = { #Niku first picks his bound land by his custom content, so skip for him
			NOT = { this = character:792736 }
			current_date >= 1230.2.2
		}
		NOR = {
			has_trait = fallen
			has_variable = host
			has_variable = stone_prison
			has_variable = earth_bound
		}
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2900
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_demon_maintenance.10.option.a
		trigger = { 
			NOT = { has_trait = fallen } 
			NOT = { has_trait = earthbound } 
		}
		
		POD_create_demon_vessel_effect = yes
		
		add_internal_flag = dangerous
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2900
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_demon_maintenance.10.option.b
		
		custom_tooltip = earthbound_artifact_choice_tt
		custom_tooltip = POD_demon_maintenance.10.option.b.tt
		
		add_trait = earthbound
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2902
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_demon_maintenance.10.option.c
		
		add_trait = earthbound
		custom_tooltip = POD_demon_maintenance.10.option.b.tt
		custom_tooltip = POD_demon_maintenance.10.option.c.tt
		set_variable = {
			name = earth_bound
			value = capital_county
		}
		capital_county = { 
			add_county_modifier = earthbound_modifier 
			set_variable = { name = reliquarie value = root } 
		}
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.2900
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.h
		
		trigger = { exists = scope:character_being_changed }
		
		remove_trait = demon
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
	}	
}

POD_chargen.2900 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.2900.t
	desc = POD_chargen.2900.desc

	theme = demon
	
	trigger = { NOT = { religion = { is_in_family = rf_demons } } }

	on_trigger_fail = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = yes }
			change_government = infernal_court_government
		}
		POD_demon_initialization_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3000
		}
		else = {
			trigger_event = { 
				id = POD_chargen.3000
				months = 1 # Timing to allow game give dynast to player
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.a
		
		set_character_faith = faith:avarice	
	}
			
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.b
		
		set_character_faith = faith:envy		
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.c
		
		set_character_faith = faith:gluttony	
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.d
		
		set_character_faith = faith:lust
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.e
		
		set_character_faith = faith:pride
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.f
		
		set_character_faith = faith:sloth
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.2900.option.g
		
		set_character_faith = faith:wrath
	}
	
	after = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = yes }
			change_government = infernal_court_government
		}
		POD_demon_initialization_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3000
		}
		else = {
			trigger_event = { 
				id = POD_chargen.3000
				months = 1 # Timing to allow game give dynast to player
			}
		}
	}
}

POD_chargen.3000 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.3000.t
	desc = POD_chargen.3000.desc

	theme = demon
	
	trigger = { 
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = dynasty }
			NOR = { 
				dynasty = dynasty:14000001
				dynasty = dynasty:14000002
				dynasty = dynasty:14000003
				dynasty = dynasty:14000004
				dynasty = dynasty:14000005
				dynasty = dynasty:14000006
				dynasty = dynasty:14000007
			}
		}
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		POD_demon_house_initialization_effect = yes
	}
	
	immediate = {		
		
		dynasty:14000001 = {
			dynast = { save_scope_as = asharu }
		}	
		dynasty:14000002 = {
			dynast = { save_scope_as = rabisu }
		}	
		dynasty:14000003 = {
			dynast = { save_scope_as = halaku }
		}	
		dynasty:14000004 = {
			dynast = { save_scope_as = namaru }
		}	
		dynasty:14000005 = {
			dynast = { save_scope_as = lammasu }
		}	
		dynasty:14000006 = {
			dynast = { save_scope_as = annunaki }
		}	
		dynasty:14000007 = {
			dynast = { save_scope_as = neberu }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			house = { save_scope_as = coa_target }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3000.option.a
		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3000.option.a.tt
		
		set_house = scope:asharu.house
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				root = character:792729
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				location = { squared_distance = { target = scope:asharu.location value <= squared_distance_small } }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 15
				location = { squared_distance = { target = scope:asharu.location value <= squared_distance_medium } }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				location = { squared_distance = { target = scope:asharu.location value <= squared_distance_large } }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 5
				location = { squared_distance = { target = scope:asharu.location value <= squared_distance_huge } }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 45
				AND = {
					exists = liege
					liege = scope:asharu
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -100
				scope:asharu = {
					is_ai = no
					max_military_strength < root.max_military_strength
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {

		custom_tooltip = POD_chargen.3000.option.b.tt

			}

			}
			modifier = {
				add = 15

			}
			modifier = {
				add = 10

			}
			modifier = {
				add = 5

			}
			modifier = {
				add = 45
				AND = {
					exists = liege
					liege = scope:rabisu
				}
			}
			modifier = {

POD_chargen.3001 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.3001.t
	desc = POD_chargen.3001.desc

	theme = demon
	
	trigger = { 
		is_from_ruler_designer = yes
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		dynasty.dynast = { set_house = scope:coa_target } # Making player dynast
		POD_demon_house_initialization_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3001.option.a
		dynasty.dynast = { set_house = scope:coa_target } # Making player dynast
		POD_demon_house_initialization_effect = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3001.option.b
		POD_create_cadet_branch_effect = yes
		dynasty.dynast = { set_house = scope:coa_target } # Making player dynast 
		POD_demon_house_initialization_effect = yes # Need repeat three time to allow cadet branch with rd coa
	}
}

# Fae
POD_chargen.3100 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.3100.t
	desc = POD_chargen.3100.desc

	theme = fae
	
	trigger = { has_kith_trigger = no }

	immediate = {
		play_music_cue = "mood_born_in_a_dream"
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = eshu }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3701
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = kithain }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3700
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = jinn }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3700
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = african_kiths }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3701
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = hsien }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3702
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = phyla }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3799
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_kithain
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3200
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_jinn
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3300
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_african_kiths
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3400
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_hsien
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3500
		
		trigger = { has_trait = changeling }
		show_as_unavailable = { has_trait = fae }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = trait_phyla
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3600
		
		trigger = { has_trait = fae }
		show_as_unavailable = { has_trait = changeling }
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3100.option.f
		trigger = { 
			has_trait = fae
			exists = scope:character_being_changed
		}
		
		remove_trait = fae
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
	}	

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.3100.option.g
		trigger = { 
			has_trait = changeling
			exists = scope:character_being_changed
		}
		
		remove_trait = changeling
		trigger_event = { id = POD_chargen.2 }
	}
}

POD_chargen.3200 = {
	type = character_event

POD_chargen.3700 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.3700.t
	desc = POD_chargen.3700.desc

	theme = fae
	
	trigger = { 
		is_a_cultist = no
		POD_is_supehunter_trigger = no
		NOR = { 
			religion = religion:winter_court_religion 
			religion = religion:spring_court_religion 
			religion = religion:summer_court_religion 
			religion = religion:autumn_court_religion 
			religion = religion:solstice_court_religion
			AND = {
				has_trait = jinn
				OR = {
					religion = religion:ojo_religion 
					religion = religion:iku_religion 
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3799
	}
	
	option = {
		name = winter_court_religion
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:winter_court_religion }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = spring_court_religion
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:spring_court_religion }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = summer_court_religion
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:summer_court_religion }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = autumn_court_religion
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:autumn_court_religion }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = solstice_court_religion
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:solstice_court_religion }
	}
	
	after = {
		POD_trigger_faith_selection_event = { NEXT_EVENT = POD_chargen.3799 }
		#trigger_event = POD_chargen.3799
		#add_character_modifier = { modifier = POD_free_faith_change_modifier days = 1 }
	}
}

POD_chargen.3701 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.3700.t
	desc = POD_chargen.3700.desc

	theme = fae
	
	trigger = { 
		is_a_cultist = no
		POD_is_supehunter_trigger = no
		NOR = { 
			religion = religion:ojo_religion 
			religion = religion:iku_religion 
			AND = {
				has_trait = eshu
				OR = {
					religion = religion:winter_court_religion 
					religion = religion:spring_court_religion 
					religion = religion:summer_court_religion 
					religion = religion:autumn_court_religion 
					religion = religion:solstice_court_religion
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	on_trigger_fail = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3799
	}
	
	option = {
		name = ojo_religion
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:ojo_religion }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = iku_religion
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:iku_religion }
	}
	
	after = {
		POD_trigger_faith_selection_event = { NEXT_EVENT = POD_chargen.3799 }
		#trigger_event = POD_chargen.3799
		#add_character_modifier = { modifier = POD_free_faith_change_modifier days = 1 }
	}
}
	
POD_chargen.3702 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.3700.t
	desc = POD_chargen.3700.desc

	theme = fae
	
	trigger = { 
		is_a_cultist = no
		POD_is_supehunter_trigger = no
		NOR = { 
			religion = religion:shinma_religion 
			religion = religion:mu_religion 
		}
	}

	on_trigger_fail = {
		trigger_event = POD_chargen.3799
	}
	
	option = {
		name = shinma_religion
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:shinma_religion }
	}
	
	option = {
		name = mu_religion
		POD_setup_faith_selection_event = { RELIGION = religion:mu_religion }
	}
	
	after = {
		POD_trigger_faith_selection_event = { NEXT_EVENT = POD_chargen.3799 }
		#trigger_event = POD_chargen.3799
		#add_character_modifier = { modifier = POD_free_faith_change_modifier days = 1 }
	}
}
	
POD_chargen.3799 = {
	hidden = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = fae }
			POD_fae_initialization_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = changeling }
			POD_changeling_initialization_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = pooka }
			trigger_event = POD_chargen.3800
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			trigger_event = { 
				id = pod1230objectives_fae.100
				days = 10
			}
			trigger_event = pod1230objectives_fae.110
		}
	}
}

POD_chargen.6000 = {
	type = character_event
	content_source = dlc_POD
	title = POD_chargen.6000.t
	desc = POD_chargen.6000.desc

	theme = faith
	
	widget = {
		gui = "POD_event_window_widget_chargen_faith_selection"
		container = "foreground_shader_vfx_container"
	}

	option = {
		name = POD_chargen.6000.a
	}	
	
	after = {
		set_character_faith = var:chargen_faith_selection
		
		clear_variable_list = chargen_faiths
		remove_variable = chargen_faith_selection
		remove_variable = chargen_religion
		
		trigger_event = { saved_event_id = scope:next_event }
	}
}
